     Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__reals_Real

        Rstruct.RbaseSymbolsImpl_R__canonical__reals_Real)
     (@classical_sets.mkset
        (@order.Order.POrder.sort orderedzmod.ring_display

AnalysisIntegralDefinitions.oriented_has_integral =
fun
  (f : forall _ : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R,
       Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R)
  (a b l : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R) =>
and (AnalysisIntegralDefinitions.segment_integrable f a b)
  (@eq Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R
     (AnalysisIntegralDefinitions.oriented_integral f a b) l)

GaussianDefinitions.moment_factor =
fix moment_factor (n : nat) : Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R :=
  match n return Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R with
  | @O => Rdefinitions.IZR (BinNums.Zpos BinNums.xH)
  | @S n0 =>
      match n0 return Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R with
      | @O => Rdefinitions.IZR BinNums.Z0
      | @S m =>
          Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.Rmult (Raxioms.INR (S m))
            (moment_factor m)
      end
  end
     : forall _ : nat, Rdefinitions.RbaseSymbolsImpl.R
